color:white;display:flex;align-items:center;justify-content:center;font-weight:800;font-size:13px;flex-shrink:0;margin-top:2px;} .ai-step-content{flex:1;} .ai-step-content strong{font-size:14px;} .ai-step-content p{margin:4px 0 0;font-size:13.5px;color:var(--text-muted);line-height:1.55;} Commas MCP Connector Connect your AI directly to your Commas account using the Model Context Protocol (MCP) . This gives the AI real-time access to query your data and execute actions through 44 built-in tools. ℹ Where to find your API key Copy your live key from your Commas dashboard → Account → API Keys , or your sandbox key from the same menu in the sandbox dashboard . Claude Desktop — One-Click MCP Setup Connect Claude Desktop to your live Commas account in seconds. Claude will be able to list products, search customers, view transactions, manage subscriptions, and more — all through natural language. 1 Download the extension One extension for everyone — no API key is embedded in the file . You'll enter your key in Claude Desktop after installing. Download Extension (.mcpb) 2 Double-click the downloaded file Claude Desktop will open and show an install prompt. Click Install . 3 Paste your API key when Claude prompts for it During install, Claude Desktop asks for a key in a field labeled FanBasis API Key — FanBasis is Commas' former name, so this is expected. Paste your Commas API key (dashboard → Account → API Keys ); it's stored securely in your device's keychain — it never lives inside the extension file. 4 Start asking questions! Try "Show me all my products" or "List my recent transactions" . Requires Claude Desktop Make sure you have Claude Desktop installed before opening the extension file. Download Claude Desktop . Alternative: Manual config setup If the extension file doesn't work, you can configure manually (requires Node.js installed): Click Copy config to copy the MCP config template Replace YOUR_API_KEY with your key from Account → API Keys Open Claude Desktop → Settings → Developer → Edit Config Paste the config, save, and restart Claude Desktop Cursor — One-Click MCP Setup Add the Commas MCP server to Cursor in one click. Cursor's AI can then list your products, search customers, pull transactions, and manage subscriptions right from the editor. Requires Node.js on your machine. 1 Paste your API key (optional but recommended) From your dashboard → Account → API Keys . It never leaves this page — it's only embedded in the local install link. 2 Click Add to Cursor Add to Cursor 3 Cursor opens with an install prompt — click Install If you skipped the key, open Cursor → Settings → MCP afterwards and replace YOUR_API_KEY with your real key. 4 Start asking! Try "List my Commas products" in Cursor's chat. Alternative: Manual config setup Click Copy config to copy the MCP config Replace YOUR_API_KEY with your key from Account → API Keys Paste it into ~/.cursor/mcp.json (or Cursor → Settings → MCP → Add server ) ChatGPT — MCP Setup Coming Soon ChatGPT connects to MCP servers via HTTP. Once the Commas remote MCP endpoint is deployed: 1 Go to ChatGPT Settings → Apps → Add new app Paste the server URL: https://www.fanbasis.com/mcp 2 Authorize with your Commas account (OAuth login flow) 3 Use in Developer Mode — click + → More → Developer Mode → select Commas Grok — MCP Setup (Developer API) Coming Soon For developers building with the xAI API, add Commas as a remote MCP tool: xAI Responses API curl https://api.x.ai/v1/responses \\ -H "Authorization: Bearer $XAI_API_KEY" \\ -d '{ "model": "grok-4-1-fast-reasoning", "input": "List my Commas products", "tools": [{ "type": "mcp", "server_url": "https://www.fanbasis.com/mcp", "server_label": "fanbasis", "headers": { "x-api-key": "YOUR_KEY" } }] }' Available AI Agent Tools A selection of the 44 tools available to every connected client — read tools and write tools (write tools always ask for confirmation first): Tool API Endpoint Description list_products GET /public-api/products List all products with prices and payment links search_customers GET /public-api/customers Search customers by name, email, or phone list_transactions GET /public-api/checkout-sessions/transactions All payments, filterable by product or customer get_transaction GET /public-api/transactions/:id Single transaction details with fees and refunds list_subscribers GET /public-api/subscribers All subscribers across all products get_checkout_session GET /public-api/checkout-sessions/:id Checkout session config and pricing get_session_transactions GET /public-api/checkout-sessions/:id/transactions Transactions for a specific session get_session_subscriptions GET /public-api/checkout-sessions/:id/subscriptions Subscriptions for a specific session get_product_transactions GET /public-api/products/:id/transactions Transactions for a specific product get_product_subscriptions GET /public-api/products/:id/subscriptions Subscriptions for a specific product list_discount_codes GET /public-api/discount-codes All discount codes with usage stats get_discount_code GET /public-api/discount-codes/:id Single discount code detail get_customer_payment_methods GET /public-api/customers/:id/payment-methods Customer's saved cards (last 4 only) create_invoice POST /public-api/invoices Create an invoice and email it to the client (asks for confirmation) list_invoices GET /public-api/invoices List invoices with status, search, and date filters get_invoice GET /public-api/invoices/{id} One invoice with line items, totals, and activity send_invoice POST /public-api/invoices/{id}/resend Re-send the invoice email to its recipient void_invoice POST /public-api/invoices/{id}/void Void an open invoice (asks for confirmation) mark_invoice_paid POST /public-api/invoices/{id}/mark-paid Record an off-platform payment (asks for confirmation) ⚠ Write actions also available Beyond the read tools above, the MCP server can also perform write actions — charging a customer, refunding a transaction, creating products, creating and previewing subscription payment links, creating/updating/deleting discount codes, managing webhooks, and extending, upgrading or canceling subscriptions. Charges, refunds, cancellations, upgrades and every delete require an explicit confirmation step before they execute. Two do not: extend_subscription (which moves a billing date) and add_api_key run immediately, so treat an agent request to extend a subscription as final. Example Conversations Once connected, try these prompts with any AI agent: 📊 Sales Summary "Give me a summary of my sales this month" 👤 Customer Lookup "Look up customer john@example.com and show his purchases" 🏷️ Discount Analysis "Which discount codes have been used the most?" 🔄 Subscriber Report "Show me all active subscribers to my Pro Monthly plan" CLI Connect to CLI Your entire Commas business, one terminal away. The Commas CLI gives you — and your AI agents — full command-line access to products, payments, customers, subscriptions, discount codes, and webhooks. Everything the API can do, in single typed commands. ◆ What can the CLI do? Issue a refund, spin up a payment link, create a discount code, cancel a subscription, look up a customer — each one is a single command like commas tx refund pX9vQ . No dashboard clicks, no API boilerplate. ⚡ One-Line Operations Refunds, payment links, discounts, subscription changes — one command each, instant results. 🤖 Built for AI Agents --json output and --yes confirmation flags let Claude Code, Cursor, or any terminal agent operate your account. 🔒 Safe by Default Refunds, charges, cancellations and deletes show you a summary and ask for confirmation before anything executes — subs extend and keys add are the exceptions and run immediately. 🔑 Your Data Only Scoped to your API key — you only ever see your own account. Sandbox mode included for risk-free testing. Setup Guide From zero to your first command in about two minutes. 1. Install Node.js 20 or newer. The CLI runs on Node — grab it from nodejs.org if you don't have it. Already installed? Check with: Terminal node --version # should print v20 or higher 2. Install the Commas CLI. Terminal npm install -g commas-cli 3. Log in with your API key. Find it in your Commas dashboard — the same key the AI Agent connector uses. Your key is stored locally on your machine and only ever sent to Commas to authenticate. Terminal commas login Commas API key: ******** Logged in — 44 tools available. 4. Verify the connection. Terminal commas status Reachable: yes (v1.0.0) Environment: production API key: comm…a4f9 ✦ Test drive in sandbox first Sandbox needs its own API key, created in the sandbox dashboard and registered against your account. Check what you have with commas keys list , add one with commas keys add sandbox , then run commas env sandbox — every command now goes to the sandbox environment, so you can create test payment links and issue fake refunds safely. Switch back with commas env production . If you ask for sandbox without a sandbox key, the command stops and tells you — it never quietly falls back to production. Using the CLI A few everyday flows. Every command supports --help for full options. See your products and recent payments: Terminal commas products list commas tx list --per-page 20 commas customers search "jane@example.com" Create a payment link: Terminal commas checkout create --title "1:1 Coaching Call" --type onetime_reusable --amount 499 Returns a payment_link you can share immediately. Issue a refund — the CLI shows you exactly what will happen and asks first: Terminal commas tx refund pX9vQ --amount 25.00 --reason "Customer request" Refund $25.00 from transaction pX9vQ. Proceed? (y/N) y Launch a discount code: Terminal commas discounts create --code LAUNCH20 --type percentage --value 20 --duration once --products 1234 --max-uses 100 Send an invoice — created, numbered, and emailed to your client in one command: Terminal commas invoices send --to jane@example.com --name "Jane" --item "Consulting:500.00" Create invoice for Jane jane@example.com — 1 × Consulting @ $500.00 — total $500.00 — and EMAIL it to them Proceed? (y/N) y ✅ Invoice INV-007 created and emailed to jane@example.com. ⚠ Confirmations on destructive commands Refunds, charges, cancellations, and deletions always show a summary and ask Proceed? (y/N) before executing. Pass --yes to skip the prompt — required for scripts and AI agents, so use it deliberately. Two exceptions run immediately with no prompt: commas subs extend and commas keys add — the server never asks for confirmation on those, so treat them as final. Command Reference Every command also accepts --json for machine-readable output and --env sandbox for a one-off sandbox run. Command What it does commas login / logout Save or remove your API key commas status Server health and current configuration commas env [production|sandbox] Show or switch the default environment commas keys list Which environments have an API key registered commas keys add production|sandbox Register the API key an environment needs (prompts without echo) commas products list List your products commas products create Create a product directly → product_id + shareable payment_link commas products transactions id Per-product transaction history (numeric ID or hashid) commas invoices create / send Create an invoice and email it to your client — --item "Name:dollars[:qty]" (confirms first) commas invoices list List invoices — filter by status, search, and date ranges commas invoices get id One invoice — line items, totals, status, activity commas invoices resend id Re-send the invoice email (payment reminder) commas invoices void id Void an open invoice (confirms first) commas invoices mark-paid id Record an off-platform payment (confirms first) commas customers search " query " Find customers by name or email commas customers payment-methods id A customer's saved payment methods commas customers charge id Charge a saved payment method (confirms first) commas tx list / get id List transactions or inspect one commas tx session id Transactions for a checkout session commas tx refund id Full or partial refund (confirms first) commas subs list All subscribers with billing status commas subs product id / session id Subscriptions for a product or session commas subs cancel id --session id Cancel a subscription (confirms first) commas subs extend Extend a subscription by N days (runs immediately — no confirmation) commas subs upgrades id Upgrade tiers available for a subscription, proration pre-calculated commas subs upgrade-preview id --to svc Exact prorated charge for an upgrade — read-only commas subs upgrade id --to svc Execute the upgrade — charges prorated amount (confirms first) commas subs link-create Subscription payment link with courses, Discord roles, bumps upsells attached commas subs link-preview Preview a subscription link without creating it commas checkout create New checkout session → shareable payment link commas checkout get / delete id Inspect or delete a checkout session commas checkout update-embedded Update an embedded session (payment methods / metadata) commas checkout embedded Embedded checkout session for iframe forms (needs --creator and --product ) commas discounts list|get|create|update|delete Manage discount codes end-to-end. Creating one needs --type , --value , --duration and --products . commas webhooks list|create|delete|test Manage webhook subscriptions, send test events commas docs question Search these API docs from the terminal commas tools List every capability the server exposes commas call tool Call any tool directly — the escape hatch CLI + AI Agents The CLI is how terminal-based AI agents run your business. Claude Code, Cursor, Codex — any agent that can run shell commands can operate your Commas account with zero extra setup: install the CLI, run commas login once, and the agent takes it from there. Agent-friendly flags commas tx list --json # structured output the agent can parse commas tx refund pX9vQ --yes # non-interactive confirmation Once it's installed, try prompts like these with your agent: 📊 Revenue Report "Use the Commas CLI to pull this week's transactions and summarize revenue by product" 🔗 Instant Offer "Create a $499 payment link for my new coaching offer and give me the URL" 🏷️ Launch Campaign "Set up a 20% launch discount limited to the first 100 buyers" 📋 Subscriber Export "Export all my active subscribers to a CSV" ◆ CLI or AI Agent connector? Both talk to the same Commas backend with the same permissions. The AI Agent connector is for chat assistants — Claude, ChatGPT, Grok. The CLI is for terminals, scripts, CI, and coding agents. Use whichever fits the moment — or both. // ── THEME TOGGLE ───────────────────────────────────────────────────── (function() { var html = document.documentElement; var toggle = document.getElementById('themeToggle'); var ICONS = { dark: '☀️', light: '🌙' }; function applyTheme(theme) { html.setAttribute('data-theme', theme); try { localStorage.setItem('fb-docs-theme', theme); } catch(e) {} if (toggle) toggle.textContent = ICONS[theme] || '🌙'; toggle && toggle.setAttribute('title', theme === 'dark' ? TAB_GROUPS[tab].label : tab; }); Array.from(resultsContainer.querySelectorAll('.cmdkGroup')).forEach(function(g) { g.remove(); }); Object.keys(TAB_GROUPS).forEach(function(tab) { if (!byTab[tab]) return; var grp = document.createElement('div'); grp.className = 'cmdkGroup'; grp.setAttribute('data-group-block', ''); var lbl = document.createElement('span'); lbl.className = 'cmdkGroupLabel'; lbl.textContent = TAB_GROUPS[tab].label; grp.appendChild(lbl); byTab[tab].forEach(function(item) { grp.appendChild(item); }); resultsContainer.appendChild(grp); }); })(); function filterCmdk(query) { var q = query.toLowerCase().trim(); var groups = resultsContainer.querySelectorAll('.cmdkGroup'); if (!q) { // Show default nav items, hide full-text results groups.forEach(function(grp) { grp.style.display = ''; }); groups.forEach(function(grp) { grp.querySelectorAll('.cmdkItem').forEach(function(item) { item.style.display = ''; }); }); // Remove any dynamic results var dynGroup = document.getElementById('cmdkDynamicResults'); if (dynGroup) dynGroup.remove(); emptyEl.style.display = 'none'; selectedIdx = -1; updateSelectedItem(); return; } // First: filter existing nav items as before var visibleCount = 0; groups.forEach(function(grp) { if (grp.id === 'cmdkDynamicResults') return; var hasVisible = false; grp.querySelectorAll('.cmdkItem').forEach(function(item) { var label = item.querySelector('.cmdkItemLabel').textContent.toLowerCase(); var sub = item.querySelector('.cmdkItemSub').textContent.toLowerCase(); var visible = label.includes(q) || sub.includes(q); item.style.display = visible ? '' : 'none'; if (visible) { hasVisible = true; visibleCount++; } }); grp.style.display = hasVisible ? '' : 'none'; }); // Second: full-text search across all indexed content var dynGroup = document.getElementById('cmdkDynamicResults'); if (dynGroup) dynGroup.remove(); var matches = []; var seen = {}; _searchIndex.forEach(function(entry) { var key = entry.anchorId + '|' + entry.title.slice(0, 40); if (seen[key]) return; var titleMatch = entry.title.toLowerCase().includes(q); var contextMatch = entry.context.toLowerCase().includes(q); if (titleMatch || contextMatch) { seen[key] = true; matches.push(entry); } }); if (matches.length > 0) { // Limit to top 15 results, headings first matches.sort(function(a, b) { if (a.type === 'heading' && b.type !== 'heading') return -1; if (b.type === 'heading' && a.type !== 'heading') return 1; return 0; }); matches = matches.slice(0, 15); var html = ' '; html += ' Content Matches '; matches.forEach(function(m) { var icon = m.type === 'heading' ? '📄' : '🔍'; var escapedTitle = m.title.replace(/ /g, ' '); var escapedContext = m.context.replace(/ /g, ' '); // Highlight match in title var re = new RegExp('(' + q.replace(/[.*+?^${}()|[\\]\\\\]/g, '\\\\$&') + ')', 'gi'); escapedTitle = escapedTitle.replace(re, ' $1 '); html += ' '; html += ' ' + icon + ' '; html += ' ' + escapedTitle + ' '; html += ' ' + cmdkCrumb(m.sectionId) + ' '; html += ' '; }); html += ' '; resultsContainer.insertAdjacentHTML('beforeend', html); visibleCount += matches.length; } emptyEl.style.display = visibleCount === 0 ? '' : 'none'; selectedIdx = -1; updateSelectedItem(); } function updateSelectedItem() { var visible = Array.from(resultsContainer.querySelectorAll('.cmdkItem')) .filter(function(el) { return el.style.display !== 'none'; }); visible.forEach(function(item, i) { item.classList.toggle('selected', i === selectedIdx); if (i === selectedIdx) { item.scrollIntoView({ block: 'nearest' }); } }); } function navigateCmdk(dir) { var visible = Array.from(resultsContainer.querySelectorAll('.cmdkItem')) .filter(function(el) { return el.style.display !== 'none'; }); if (!visible.length) return; selectedIdx = Math.max(-1, Math.min(visible.length - 1, selectedIdx + dir)); if (selectedIdx === -1) selectedIdx = dir > 0 ? 0 : visible.length - 1; updateSelectedItem(); } function activateCmdk() { var visible = Array.from(resultsContainer.querySelectorAll('.cmdkItem')) .filter(function(el) { return el.style.display !== 'none'; }); var item = selectedIdx >= 0 ? visible[selectedIdx] : visible[0]; if (item) { var secId = item.dataset.section; var anchorId = item.dataset.anchor || null; if (window._fbShowSection) { // If there's a specific anchor, pass it as scroll target window._fbShowSection(secId, anchorId && anchorId !== secId ? anchorId : null); } closeCmdk(); } } if (overlay) { overlay.addEventListener('click', function(e) { if (e.target === overlay) closeCmdk(); }); } if (input) { input.addEventListener('input', function() { filterCmdk(input.value); }); input.addEventListener('keydown', function(e) { if (e.key === 'ArrowDown') { e.preventDefault(); navigateCmdk(1); } else if (e.key === 'ArrowUp') { e.preventDefault(); navigateCmdk(-1); } else if (e.key === 'Enter') { e.preventDefault(); activateCmdk(); } else if (e.key === 'Escape') closeCmdk(); }); } // Wire up result item clicks if (resultsContainer) { resultsContainer.addEventListener('click', function(e) { var item = e.target.closest('.cmdkItem'); if (item) { var secId = item.dataset.section; var anchorId = item.dataset.anchor || null; if (window._fbShowSection) { window._fbShowSection(secId, anchorId && anchorId !== secId ? anchorId : null); } closeCmdk(); } }); } // Keyboard shortcuts document.addEventListener('keydown', function(e) { // CMD+K or CTRL+K if ((e.metaKey || e.ctrlKey) && e.key === 'k') { e.preventDefault(); if (overlay && overlay.classList.contains('open')) closeCmdk(); else openCmdk(); return; } // ESC if (e.key === 'Escape' && overlay && overlay.classList.contains('open')) { closeCmdk(); return; } // / to open search (only if not in an input) var tag = document.activeElement.tagName; if (e.key === '/' && tag !== 'INPUT' && tag !== 'TEXTAREA') { e.preventDefault(); openCmdk(); } }); }); // end ready })(); // ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════ // SUPERCHARGE — Mintlify-level features // ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════ (function() { function ready(fn) { if (document.readyState !== 'loading') fn(); else document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', fn); } // ─── CURL PARSER — extracts method, url, headers, body ── function parseCurl(code) { code = code.trim(); var method = 'GET'; var mMatch = code.match(/-X\\s+([A-Z]+)/); if (mMatch) method = mMatch[1]; else if (code.includes('-d ') || code.includes("--data")) method = 'POST'; var urlMatch = code.match(/curl\\s+(?:-X\\s+\\w+\\s+)?(?:-s\\s+)?['"]?(https?:\\/\\/[^\\s'"\\\\]+)['"]?/); var url = urlMatch ? urlMatch[1] : ''; var shortUrl = url.replace('https://www.fanbasis.com', '').replace('https://fanbasis.com', ''); // Extract headers var headers = {}; var hMatches = [...code.matchAll(/-H\\s+["']([^"']+)["']/g)]; hMatches.forEach(function(m) { var parts = m[1].split(/:\\s*(.+)/); if (parts.length >= 2) headers[parts[0].trim()] = parts[1].trim(); }); // Extract body var bodyMatch = code.match(/-d\\s+['"](\\{[\\s\\S]*?\\})['"]/) || code.match(/--data\\s+['"](\\{[\\s\\S]*?\\})['"]/) || code.match(/-d\\s+'([\\s\\S]+?)'(?:\\s*$|\\s*\\\\)/m); var body = bodyMatch ? bodyMatch[1].trim() : null; return { method: method, url: url, shortUrl: shortUrl, headers: headers, body: body }; } // ─── CODE GENERATOR ───────────────────────────────────── function genPython(parsed) { var lines = []; lines.push('import requests'); lines.push(''); if (parsed.headers['Content-Type']) { lines.push('headers = {'); Object.entries(parsed.headers).forEach(function(e) { lines.push(' "' + e[0] + '": "' + e[1] + '",'); }); lines.push('}'); } else { lines.push('headers = {"x-api-key": "YOUR_API_KEY"}'); } if (parsed.body) { lines.push(''); try { var parsed_body = JSON.parse(parsed.body); lines.push('payload = ' + JSON.stringify(parsed_body, null, 4).replace(/^/gm, '').replace(/\ /g, '\ ')); } catch(e) { lines.push('payload = ' + parsed.body); } lines.push(''); lines.push('response = requests.' + parsed.method.toLowerCase() + '('); lines.push(' "' + parsed.url + '",'); lines.push(' headers=headers,'); lines.push(' json=payload'); lines.push(')'); } else { lines.push(''); lines.push('response = requests.' + parsed.method.toLowerCase() + '('); lines.push(' "' + parsed.url + '",'); lines.push(' headers=headers'); lines.push(')'); } lines.push(''); lines.push('print(response.json())'); return lines.join('\ '); } function genNode(parsed) { var lines = []; var bodyStr = parsed.body ? JSON.stringify(JSON.parse(parsed.body || '{}'), null, 2) : null; lines.push('const response = await fetch("' + parsed.url + '", {'); lines.push(' method: "' + parsed.method + '",'); lines.push(' headers: {'); if (Object.keys(parsed.headers).length) { Object.entries(parsed.headers).forEach(function(e) { lines.push(' "' + e[0] + '": "' + e[1] + '",'); }); } else { lines.push(' "x-api-key": "YOUR_API_KEY",'); } lines.push(' },'); if (bodyStr) { lines.push(' body: JSON.stringify(' + bodyStr.replace(/\ /g, '\ ') + '),'); } lines.push('});'); lines.push(''); lines.push('const data = await response.json();'); lines.push('console.log(data);'); return lines.join('\ '); } function genPhp(parsed) { var lines = []; lines.push(' "' + parsed.url + '",'); lines.push(' CURLOPT_RETURNTRANSFER => true,'); if (parsed.method !== 'GET') { lines.push(' CURLOPT_CUSTOMREQUEST => "' + parsed.method + '",'); } lines.push(' CURLOPT_HTTPHEADER => ['); var hdrs = parsed.headers; if (Object.keys(hdrs).length) { Object.entries(hdrs).forEach(function(e) { lines.push(' "' + e[0] + ': ' + e[1] + '",'); }); } else { lines.push(' "x-api-key: YOUR_API_KEY",'); } lines.push(' ],'); if (parsed.body) { lines.push(' CURLOPT_POSTFIELDS => \\'' + parsed.body.replace(/\\\\/g, '\\\\\\\\').replace(/'/g, "\\\\'") + '\\','); } lines.push(']);'); lines.push(''); lines.push('$response = curl_exec($ch);'); lines.push('curl_close($ch);'); lines.push(''); lines.push('$data = json_decode($response, true);'); lines.push('print_r($data);'); return lines.join('\ '); } function genGo(parsed) { var lines = []; lines.push('package main'); lines.push(''); lines.push('import ('); lines.push(' "fmt"'); lines.push(' "net/http"'); if (parsed.body) lines.push(' "strings"'); lines.push(' "io"'); lines.push(')'); lines.push(''); lines.push('func main() {'); if (parsed.body) { lines.push(' payload := strings.NewReader(`' + parsed.body + '`)'); lines.push(' req, _ := http.NewRequest("' + parsed.method + '", "' + parsed.url + '", payload)'); } else { lines.push(' req, _ := http.NewRequest("' + parsed.method + '", "' + parsed.url + '", nil)'); } Object.entries(parsed.headers).forEach(function(e) { lines.push(' req.Header.Set("' + e[0] + '", "' + e[1] + '")'); }); lines.push(' client := &http.Client{}'); lines.push(' res, _ := client.Do(req)'); lines.push(' defer res.Body.Close()'); lines.push(' body, _ := io.ReadAll(res.Body)'); lines.push(' fmt.Println(string(body))'); lines.push('}'); return lines.join('\ '); } // ─── SYNTAX HIGHLIGHTER (JS + JSON + Shell) ───────────── function syntaxHL(code, lang) { // Escape HTML first var escaped = code .replace(/&/g, ' ') .replace(/ /g, ' '); if (lang === 'json' || (lang === 'auto' && /^\\s*[\\[{]/.test(code.trim()))) { // JSON highlighting return escaped .replace(/("(?:[^"\\\\]|\\\\.)*")\\s*:/g, ' $1 :') .replace(/:\\s*("(?:[^"\\\\]|\\\\.)*")/g, ': $1 ') .replace(/:\\s*(\\d+(?:\\.\\d+)?)/g, ': $1 ') .replace(/:\\s*(true|false)/g, ': $1 ') .replace(/:\\s*(null)/g, ': $1 '); } if (lang === 'shell' || lang === 'curl') { return escaped // URL .replace(/(https?:\\/\\/[^\\s\\\\'"&]+)/g, ' $1 ') // Flags .replace(/(\\s-[Xsd](?=\\s)|\\s--data(?=\\s)|\\s--header(?=\\s))/g, ' $1 ') // Header names (after -H) .replace(/(-H\\s+ | )([A-Za-z-]+)(?=:)/g, '$1 $2 ') // Strings in quotes .replace(/ ([^&]*) /g, ' $1 ') // curl command itself .replace(/^(curl)/gm, ' $1 '); } if (lang === 'python') { return escaped .replace(/(import|from|def|class|return|if|else|for|while|with|as|print)\\b/g, ' $1 ') .replace(/(#.*)$/gm, ' $1 ') .replace(/("(?:[^"\\\\]|\\\\.)*"|'(?:[^'\\\\]|\\\\.)*')/g, ' $1 ') .replace(/\\b(\\d+)\\b/g, ' $1 '); } if (lang === 'javascript' || lang === 'node') { return escaped .replace(/\\b(const|let|var|function|return|await|async|if|else|for|while|import|export|from|new)\\b/g, ' $1 ') .replace(/(\\/\\/.*)$/gm, ' $1 ') .replace(/(`(?:[^`\\\\]|\\\\.)*`|"(?:[^"\\\\]|\\\\.)*"|'(?:[^'\\\\]|\\\\.)*')/g, ' $1 ') .replace(/\\b(\\d+)\\b/g, ' $1 '); } if (lang === 'php') { return escaped .replace(/( \\?php)/g, ' $1 ') .replace(/\\b(echo|print_r|var_dump|curl_init|curl_setopt_array|curl_exec|curl_close|json_decode|json_encode|true|false|null)\b/g, ' $1 ') .replace(/\\b(CURLOPT_\\w+)\b/g, ' $1 ') .replace(/(#[^\ ]*)$/gm, ' $1 ') .replace(/("(?:[^"\\\\]|\\\\.)*"|'(?:[^'\\\\]|\\\\.)*')/g, ' $1 ') .replace(/\\b(\\d+)\\b/g, ' $1 '); } return escaped; } // ─── COPY TO CLIPBOARD HELPER ─────────────────────────── function copyText(text, btn, successText) { navigator.clipboard.writeText(text).then(function() { var orig = btn.innerHTML; btn.innerHTML = (successText || '✓ Copied!'); btn.classList.add('copied', 'copied-state', 'copied-flash'); setTimeout(function() { btn.innerHTML = orig; btn.classList.remove('copied', 'copied-state', 'copied-flash'); }, 1800); }).catch(function() { // Fallback var ta = document.createElement('textarea'); ta.value = text; ta.style.cssText = 'position:fixed;top:-9999px'; document.body.appendChild(ta); ta.select(); document.execCommand('copy'); document.body.removeChild(ta); }); } ready(function() { // ── 1. MULTI-LANGUAGE CODE TABS ────────────────────── document.querySelectorAll('.code-block').forEach(function(block) { var label = block.querySelector('.code-label'); if (!label) return; if (!label.classList.contains('request')) return; var pre = block.querySelector('pre code') || block.querySelector('pre'); if (!pre) return; var rawCode = pre.textContent || ''; if (!rawCode.trim().startsWith('curl')) return; // Parse the curl var parsed; try { parsed = parseCurl(rawCode); } catch(e) { return; } // Generate tab content var shellCode = rawCode.trim(); var pythonCode = genPython(parsed); var nodeCode = genNode(parsed); // Build tabs UI var wrap = document.createElement('div'); wrap.className = 'lang-tabs-block'; var bar = document.createElement('div'); bar.className = 'lang-tabs-bar'; bar.innerHTML = ' Shell ' + ' Python ' + ' Node.js ' + ' PHP '; var panels = document.createElement('div'); panels.className = 'lang-panels'; function makePanel(lang, code, hlLang) { var div = document.createElement('div'); div.className = 'lang-panel' + (lang === 'shell' ? ' active' : ''); div.dataset.lang = lang; var pre = document.createElement('pre'); var codeEl = document.createElement('code'); codeEl.setAttribute('data-template', code); codeEl.setAttribute('data-lang', hlLang || lang); codeEl.innerHTML = syntaxHL(code, hlLang || lang); pre.appendChild(codeEl); div.appendChild(pre); return div; } panels.appendChild(makePanel('shell', shellCode, 'shell')); panels.appendChild(makePanel('python', pythonCode, 'python')); panels.appendChild(makePanel('node', nodeCode, 'javascript')); var phpCode = genPhp(parsed); panels.appendChild(makePanel('php', phpCode, 'php')); // Copy button var copyBtn = document.createElement('button'); copyBtn.className = 'code-copy-float'; copyBtn.innerHTML = '⎘ Copy'; copyBtn.addEventListener('click', function() { var activePanel = panels.querySelector('.lang-panel.active'); var activeCode = activePanel ? activePanel.querySelector('code').textContent : shellCode; copyText(activeCode, copyBtn, '✓ Copied!'); }); wrap.appendChild(bar); wrap.appendChild(panels); wrap.appendChild(copyBtn); // Tab switching bar.querySelectorAll('.lang-tab').forEach(function(tab) { tab.addEventListener('click', function() { bar.querySelectorAll('.lang-tab').forEach(function(t) { t.classList.remove('active'); }); panels.querySelectorAll('.lang-panel').forEach(function(p) { p.classList.remove('active'); }); tab.classList.add('active'); var panel = panels.querySelector('[data-lang="' + tab.dataset.lang + '"]'); if (panel) panel.classList.add('active'); }); }); // ── TRY IT PANEL ──────────────────────────────────────────── (function() { // Button lives in the tab bar, pushed to the far right var tryBtn = document.createElement('button'); tryBtn.className = 'try-it-btn'; tryBtn.textContent = '▶ Try it'; bar.appendChild(tryBtn); // Panel sits below the lang-panels inside wrap var tryPanel = document.createElement('div'); tryPanel.className = 'try-it-panel'; // URL row: method badge + short URL var urlRow = document.createElement('div'); urlRow.className = 'try-it-url-row'; var shortUrl = parsed.url.replace('https://www.fanbasis.com', ''); urlRow.innerHTML = ' ' + parsed.method + ' ' + ' ' + shortUrl + ' '; tryPanel.appendChild(urlRow); // API key input — the docs example ships a placeholder key; a real one is // required or every request would 401. Shared across panels, never stored. var keyWrap = document.createElement('div'); var keyLbl = document.createElement('div'); keyLbl.className = 'try-it-section-label'; keyLbl.textContent = 'API Key'; var keyInput = document.createElement('input'); keyInput.type = 'password'; keyInput.className = 'try-it-key'; keyInput.placeholder = 'Paste your Commas API key (kept in memory only)'; keyInput.autocomplete = 'off'; keyInput.value = window._fbTryItKey || ''; keyInput.addEventListener('input', function() { window._fbTryItKey = keyInput.value; }); keyWrap.appendChild(keyLbl); keyWrap.appendChild(keyInput); tryPanel.appendChild(keyWrap); // Body textarea — only for requests that have a body var bodyArea = null; if (parsed.body) { var bodyWrap = document.createElement('div'); var bodyLbl = document.createElement('div'); bodyLbl.className = 'try-it-section-label'; bodyLbl.textContent = 'Request Body'; bodyArea = document.createElement('textarea'); bodyArea.className = 'try-it-body'; bodyArea.spellcheck = false; try { bodyArea.value = JSON.stringify(JSON.parse(parsed.body), null, 2); } catch(e) { bodyArea.value = parsed.body; } // Dynamically size to content (up to ~280px) bodyArea.rows = Math.min(16, Math.max(5, (bodyArea.value.match(/\ /g) || []).length + 2)); bodyWrap.appendChild(bodyLbl); bodyWrap.appendChild(bodyArea); tryPanel.appendChild(bodyWrap); } // Send button + response area var sendBtn = document.createElement('button'); sendBtn.className = 'try-it-send'; sendBtn.textContent = '▶ Send Request'; var responseArea = document.createElement('div'); responseArea.className = 'try-it-response'; sendBtn.addEventListener('click', function() { var key = (keyInput.value || '').trim(); if (!key) { responseArea.style.display = 'block'; responseArea.innerHTML = ' Enter your API key above to send a live request. You can find it in your Commas dashboard. '; keyInput.focus(); return; } var hdrs = {}; Object.entries(parsed.headers).forEach(function(kv) { hdrs[kv[0]] = kv[1]; }); hdrs['x-api-key'] = key; var fetchOpts = { method: parsed.method, headers: hdrs }; if (bodyArea) { hdrs['Content-Type'] = 'application/json'; fetchOpts.body = bodyArea.value; } sendBtn.textContent = '⏳ Sending…'; sendBtn.disabled = true; responseArea.innerHTML = ''; fetch(parsed.url, fetchOpts) .then(function(res) { var status = res.status; return res.text().then(function(text) { return { status: status, body: text }; }); }) .then(function(result) { var body = result.body; try { body = JSON.stringify(JSON.parse(result.body), null, 2); } catch(e) {} var cls = result.status ' + ' ' + result.status + ' ' + ' Response ' + ' ' + ' ' + syntaxHL(body, 'json') + ' '; sendBtn.textContent = '▶ Send Request'; sendBtn.disabled = false; }) .catch(function(err) { responseArea.innerHTML = ' ' + ' Error ' + ' ' + ' ' + err.message + ' This is likely a CORS error. The API must allow requests from this page\\'s origin. '; sendBtn.textContent = '▶ Send Request'; sendBtn.disabled = false; }); }); tryPanel.appendChild(sendBtn); tryPanel.appendChild(responseArea); wrap.appendChild(tryPanel); // Toggle open/close tryBtn.addEventListener('click', function() { var isOpen = tryPanel.style.display === 'block'; tryPanel.style.display = isOpen ? 'none' : 'block'; tryBtn.classList.toggle('active', !isOpen); tryBtn.textContent = isOpen ? '▶ Try it' : '▼ Try it'; }); })(); // ── END TRY IT PANEL ───────────────────────────────────────── // Replace original block block.parentNode.insertBefore(wrap, block); block.style.display = 'none'; }); // ── 2. RESPONSE CODE HIGHLIGHTING + MULTI-STATUS TABS ──── // Standard Commas error responses for each status code var FB_ERROR_RESPONSES = { '400': JSON.stringify({ "status": "error", "message": "Validation failed. Please check the required fields.", "data": [], "errors": { "field_name": ["This field is required."] } }, null, 2), '401': JSON.stringify({ "status": "error", "message": "Invalid API key. Please provide a valid x-api-key header.", "data": [], "errors": {} }, null, 2), '422': JSON.stringify({ "status": "error", "message": "The request was well-formed but could not be processed.", "data": [], "errors": { "amount": ["Must be a positive number greater than 0."] } }, null, 2), '500': JSON.stringify({ "status": "error", "message": "An unexpected server error occurred. Please try again later.", "data": [], "errors": {} }, null, 2) }; // Label map for all status codes used across Commas endpoints var STATUS_LABEL_MAP = { '200': '200 OK', '201': '201 Created', '400': '400', '401': '401', '403': '403', '404': '404', '422': '422', '500': '500' }; // Fallback tabs when endpoint is not in FB_ENDPOINT_ERRORS var FALLBACK_TABS = ['200', '400', '401', '500']; document.querySelectorAll('.code-block').forEach(function(block) { if (block.style.display === 'none') return; var label = block.querySelector('.code-label'); var pre = block.querySelector('pre code') || block.querySelector('pre'); if (!pre) return; var rawCode = pre.textContent || ''; // ── RESPONSE BLOCKS: per-endpoint status tabs + inline copy button ── // Skip webhook event payload examples — only API response blocks get tabs var isPayload = label && /payload/i.test(label.textContent); if (label && label.classList.contains('response') && !isPayload) { var successContent = rawCode; // Syntax-highlight the success response immediately if (/^\\s*[\\[{]/.test(rawCode.trim())) { pre.innerHTML = syntaxHL(rawCode, 'json'); } // Look up per-endpoint definition var epId = block.dataset ? block.dataset.endpointId : null; var epDef = (epId && window.FB_ENDPOINT_ERRORS && window.FB_ENDPOINT_ERRORS[epId]) ? window.FB_ENDPOINT_ERRORS[epId] : null; // Determine which tabs to build var tabCodes = (epDef && epDef._tabs && epDef._tabs.length) ? epDef._tabs : FALLBACK_TABS; // First tab is the success tab (200 or 201) var successCode = tabCodes[0] || '200'; // Wrap label text in a span so we can flex-position it var labelTextContent = label.textContent.trim(); label.innerHTML = ''; label.style.cssText = 'display:flex; align-items:center; gap:6px; flex-wrap:nowrap;'; var titleSpan = document.createElement('span'); titleSpan.className = 'label-title'; titleSpan.textContent = labelTextContent; label.appendChild(titleSpan); // Spacer var spacer = document.createElement('span'); spacer.className = 'label-spacer'; label.appendChild(spacer); // Build status tabs bar var tabsBar = document.createElement('div'); tabsBar.className = 'res-status-tabs'; tabCodes.forEach(function(code) { // Skip error tabs that have no real content defined if (code !== successCode && !(epDef && epDef[code])) return; var btn = document.createElement('button'); btn.className = 'res-status-tab s' + code; if (code === successCode) btn.classList.add('active'); btn.textContent = STATUS_LABEL_MAP[code] || code; btn.addEventListener('click', function() { // Update active tab tabsBar.querySelectorAll('.res-status-tab').forEach(function(t) { t.classList.remove('active'); }); btn.classList.add('active'); // Swap content var content; if (code === successCode) { content = successContent; } else { content = (epDef && epDef[code]) ? '?' + parts.join('&') : ''; return fetch(FANBASIS_BASE + path + qs, { method: 'GET', headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json', 'x-api-key': _apiKey } }) .then(function(r) { return r.json().then(function(d) { return { ok: r.ok, data: d }; }); }) .then(function(res) { if (!res.ok) { return { content: [{ type: 'text', text: 'Error: ' + (res.data.message || JSON.stringify(res.data)) }] }; } return { content: [{ type: 'text', text: JSON.stringify(res.data, null, 2) }] }; }) .catch(function(e) { return { content: [{ type: 'text', text: 'Request failed: ' + e.message }] }; }); } // ── Register All Tools ───────────────────────────────────────────── function registerTools(mcp) { mcp.registerTool('fanbasis_list_products', 'List all products (digital goods) for this seller. } ); } // ── Register Resource ────────────────────────────────────────────── function registerResources(mcp) { mcp.registerResource('fanbasis-seller-overview', "Quick overview of this seller's Commas account.", { uri: 'fanbasis://seller/overview', mimeType: 'application/json' }, function(uri) { return Promise.all([ fetch(FANBASIS_BASE + '/products?per_page=5', { headers: { 'x-api-key': _apiKey } }).then(function(r){return r.json();}), fetch(FANBASIS_BASE + '/checkout-sessions/transactions?per_page=5', { headers: { 'x-api-key': _apiKey } }).then(function(r){return r.json();}), fetch(FANBASIS_BASE + '/subscribers?per_page=1', { headers: { 'x-api-key': _apiKey } }).then(function(r){return r.json();}) ]).then(function(results) { var overview = { products: { total: (results[0].data && results[0].data.total) || 0 }, transactions: { total: (results[1].data && results[1].data.pagination && results[1].data.pagination.total_items) || 0 }, subscribers: { total: (results[2].data && results[2].data.pagination && results[2].data.pagination.total_items) || 0 } }; return { contents: [{ uri: uri, mimeType: 'application/json', text: JSON.stringify(overview, null, 2) }] }; }); } ); } // ── Activation Function (called by the button) ───────────────────── window.activateFanBasisMCP = function() { var input = document.getElementById('mcp-api-key-input'); var status = document.getElementById('mcp-status'); var btn = document.getElementById('mcp-activate-btn'); var key = input ? input.value.trim() : ''; if (!key) { status.innerHTML = ' Please enter your API key first. '; return; } // Show loading state status.innerHTML = ' Loading WebMCP library… '; if (btn) { btn.textContent = 'Loading…'; btn.style.opacity = '0.6'; } // Load WebMCP on demand (avoids blocking page load) loadWebMCP(function(err) { if (err || typeof WebMCP === 'undefined') { status.innerHTML = ' ' + (err || 'WebMCP library failed to load.') + ' Please check your connection and try again. '; if (btn) { btn.textContent = 'Retry Activation'; btn.style.opacity = '1'; } _webmcpError = false; // allow retry return; } _apiKey = key; // Initialize WebMCP _mcp = new WebMCP({ color: '#FD3363', position: 'bottom-right', size: '40px', padding: '15px' }); registerTools(_mcp); registerPrompts(_mcp); registerResources(_mcp); status.innerHTML = ' ✓ AI Agent activated! Look for the purple widget in the bottom-right corner. Connect your MCP client by pasting a token.'; // Disable the button to prevent double-init if (btn) { btn.textContent = 'Activated ✓'; btn.style.opacity = '0.6'; btn.style.cursor = 'default'; btn.onclick = null; } // Mask the API key input input.type = 'password'; input.disabled = true; }); }; })(); // ── AI Agent Tab Switching & Copy Helpers ────────────────────────────── function toggleAIDropdown(e) { e.stopPropagation(); var wrap = document.getElementById('aiDropdownWrap'); if (!wrap) return; var aiOpen = wrap.classList.toggle('open'); var aiTriggers = wrap.querySelectorAll('.fb-ai-btn-main, .fb-ai-btn-caret'); for (var i = 0; i Commas is a payment processor for digital products. This document covers the complete REST API for managing checkout sessions, transactions, customers, subscriptions, products, discount codes, and webhooks.\ \ **Base URL:** `https://www.fanbasis.com` (paths shown below include the `/public-api/...` prefix)\ \ **Authentication:** Include your API key in the `x-api-key` header on every request.\ \ **Scopes:** keys carry scopes; a key missing one gets `403` with `\\"API key does not have permission for this resource. Required scope(s): \\"`. Map: `checkout-sessions` \\u2192 `/checkout-sessions/*` (except refund), `/products/*`, `/discount-codes/*`; `refunds` \\u2192 the refund endpoint; `payments` \\u2192 `/transactions/*`; `webhooks` \\u2192 `/webhook-subscriptions/*`; `customers` \\u2192 `/customers/*`, `/subscribers`; `subscriptions` \\u2192 the Subscription Proration endpoints. Default keys carry all scopes.\ \ ```\ curl https://www.fanbasis.com/public-api/products \\\\\ -H \\"x-api-key: YOUR_API_KEY\\"\ ```\ \ **Pagination:** Most list endpoints support `page` and `per_page` query parameters (default: 10 per page). There are no `sort`/`order` parameters. Results come back as `data. []` plus `data.pagination { current_page, per_page, total_items, total_pages, has_more }` \\u2014 except `GET /products` and `GET /discount-codes`, which return a raw Laravel paginator (`data.current_page`, `data.data[]`, `data.total`).\ \ ---\ \ ## Products\ \ ### List Products\ ```\ GET /public-api/products\ ```\ Returns all products associated with your account, including prices, payment links, and product metadata.\ \ **Response fields:** `id` (hashid), `title`, `internal_name`, `description`, `price` (string, in dollars), `payment_link`. Community & Courses products are excluded. Returns a raw Laravel paginator (`data.current_page`, `data.data[]`, `data.total`).\ \ Also available: `POST /public-api/products/create` (takes `price` in DOLLARS, min 1 \\u2014 not cents), `GET /public-api/products/:id/transactions` and `GET /public-api/products/:id/subscriptions` (NUMERIC product ids only \\u2014 hashids are not decoded on these routes), `GET /public-api/transactions/all` (refunds on rows from this endpoint may show zeroed amounts).\ \ ---\ \ ## Customers\ \ ### List / Search Customers\ ```\ GET /public-api/customers?search={query}\ ```\ Search customers by name, email, or phone number. Returns customer profiles with contact information.\ \ **Query parameters:**\ - `search` \\u2014 Filter by name, email, or phone\ - `page`, `per_page` \\u2014 Pagination\ \ **Response fields:** `id` (numeric integer), `name`, `email`, `phone`, `country_code`, `total_transactions`, `total_spent` (may serialize as a string), `last_transaction_date`\ \ ### Get Customer Payment Methods\ ```\ GET /public-api/customers/:customerId/payment-methods\ ```\ Returns saved payment methods for a customer (card last 4 digits, brand, expiry only \\u2014 no full card numbers).\ \ ### Charge a Customer\ ```\ POST /public-api/customers/:customerId/charge\ ```\ Charge a customer using a saved payment method.\ \ **Body parameters:** `payment_method_id` (string, REQUIRED), `amount_cents` (integer, REQUIRED, min 1) \\u2014 amount in cents, `description` (string, REQUIRED, max 255), `metadata` (object, optional \\u2014 string values only, and NOT persisted). Supports `Idempotency-Key` (replay within 10 minutes returns 409 \\"This charge request was already processed.\\"). Undocumented preconditions: manual rebilling must be enabled for the organization, and the customer needs a prior non-free purchase plus an authorized payment method (charge_consent). `customerId` is the raw numeric customer id.\ \ ---\ \ ## Checkout Sessions\ \ ### Create Checkout Session\ ```\ POST /public-api/checkout-sessions\ ```\ Create a new checkout session. Returns a `checkout_session_id` and `payment_link` for the buyer.\ \ **Body parameters:**\ - `product.title` (string, required), `product.description` (string, optional)\ - `amount_cents` (integer, required) \\u2014 price in cents, **minimum 100** ($1.00)\ - `type` (string, required) \\u2014 one of `subscription`, `onetime_reusable`, `onetime_non_reusable`\ - `metadata` (object, optional \\u2014 values must be STRINGS; only the reserved key `allowed_payment_methods` takes an array), `success_url` (uri, optional), `webhook_url` (uri, optional), `expiration_date` (date, optional)\ - For subscriptions: `subscription.frequency_days` (required), `subscription.free_trial_days`, `subscription.auto_expire_after_x_periods`, `subscription.initial_fee` (min 1 \\u2014 cannot be combined with `free_trial_days`), `subscription.initial_fee_days` (all optional)\ \ **Type guide:**\ - `subscription` \\u2014 recurring billing every `frequency_days`\ - `onetime_reusable` \\u2014 single-charge link, unlimited buyers (good for digital products)\ - `onetime_non_reusable` \\u2014 single-charge link, expires after first payment (good for custom invoices)\ \ **Response:** `{ \\"id\\": \\"NLxj6\\", \\"checkout_session_id\\": 345424, \\"payment_link\\": \\"https://...\\" }`\ \ ### Get Checkout Session\ ```\ GET /public-api/checkout-sessions/:checkoutSessionId\ ```\ Retrieve details of a specific checkout session including configuration and pricing.\ \ ### Delete Checkout Session\ ```\ DELETE /public-api/checkout-sessions/:checkoutSessionId\ ```\ Delete/archive a checkout session.\ \ ### Create Embedded Checkout Session\ ```\ POST /public-api/checkout-sessions/embedded\ ```\ Create an embedded checkout session for iframe-based payment forms.\ \ **Body parameters:** `metadata` (object, optional) \\u2014 echoed in webhooks. (`creator_id` and `product_id` are accepted but not validated or used \\u2014 the session is scoped by your API key and the secret is reusable across products.)\ \ **Response:** `{ \\"id\\", \\"checkout_session_secret\\", \\"metadata\\", \\"allowed_payment_methods\\", \\"created_at\\" }`\ \ ### Update Embedded Checkout Session\ ```\ PATCH /public-api/checkout-sessions/embedded/:checkoutSessionId\ ```\ Update an embedded checkout session's metadata or allowed payment methods after creation. `checkoutSessionId` is the session's `checkout_session_secret`. Shallow-merges into existing metadata — only supplied keys are updated; `metadata: null` or `{}` is a no-op.\ \ ---\ \ ## Transactions\ \ ### List All Transactions\ ```\ GET /public-api/checkout-sessions/transactions?page=1&per_page=20\ ```\ List all transactions across all checkout sessions. Supports pagination.\ \ **Response fields:** `id`, `transaction_date`, `fan` (object: id, name, email, phone, country_code), `service` and `product` (objects: id, title, price, payment_link), `servicePayment` (object: payment_type, fund_release_on, fund_released), `amount`, `fee_amount`, `net_amount` (all in dollars), `refunds` (array)\ \ ### Get Single Transaction\ ```\ GET /public-api/transactions/:transactionId\ ```\ Get detailed information about a specific transaction including fees, refund status, and payment method.\ \ ### Get Transactions for a Checkout Session\ ```\ GET /public-api/checkout-sessions/:checkoutSessionId/transactions?page=1&per_page=20\ ```\ List all transactions associated with a specific checkout session.\ \ ### Refund a Transaction\ ```\ POST /public-api/checkout-sessions/transactions/:transactionId/refund\ ```\ Issue a full or partial refund for a transaction. Refunds are synchronous \\u2014 a 200 means the refund is done; there is no status lifecycle. The path accepts the transaction hashid or the public order ID (`ORD-XXXX-XXXX-XXXX`).\ \ **Body parameters:** `amount_cents` (integer, REQUIRED, min 1 \\u2014 pass the full remaining amount for a full refund), `reason` (string, REQUIRED, 3-255 chars). An empty body returns 400 Validation failed.\ \ **Response data:** `{ refund_id, transaction_id, refund_amount, refund_amount_cents, refund_type (\\"full\\"|\\"partial\\"), refund_cost, proportional_fee, creator_amount_deduction }`. Supports `Idempotency-Key` (10-minute window; replay returns 409).\ \ ---\ \ ## Subscriptions\ \ ### List All Subscribers\ ```\ GET /public-api/subscribers\ ```\ List all active subscribers across all products. Returns subscription status, billing dates, and plan details.\ \ **Response fields:** `id`, `customer` (object: id, name, email, phone, country_code), `product` (object: id, title, price, payment_link), `subscription` (object: id, status, service_type, payment_frequency, completion_date, cancelled_at, auto_renew_count, charge_consent, created_at)\ \ `?customer_id=` requires the RAW NUMERIC customer id \\u2014 the hashid returned in `customer.id` will not match. `?product_id=` accepts the product hashid.\ \ ### Get Subscriptions for a Checkout Session\ ```\ GET /public-api/checkout-sessions/:checkoutSessionId/subscriptions?page=1\ ```\ List all subscriptions associated with a specific checkout session.\ \ ### Get Subscriptions for a Product\ ```\ GET /public-api/products/:productId/subscriptions?page=1\ ```\ List all subscriptions for a specific product. `:productId` must be the NUMERIC product id — hashids are not decoded on this route.\ \ ### Cancel a Subscription\ ```\ DELETE /public-api/checkout-sessions/:checkoutSessionId/subscriptions/:subscriptionId\ ```\ Cancel a subscription. Cancellation is immediate (no further charges); access generally runs to the end of the paid period, but community access (Discord/Telegram) is revoked immediately during a free-trial or initial-fee period. Only `active` subscriptions can be cancelled \\u2014 anything else, including an already-cancelled subscription, returns `404 \\"Subscription not found or not active\\"` (not 400). `subscriptionId` is the numeric subscription id (e.g. `111388`).\ \ ### Extend a Subscription\ ```\ POST /public-api/checkout-sessions/:checkoutSessionId/extend-subscription\ ```\ Push the next billing date forward by a set number of days (e.g., for gifting extra time).\ \ **Body parameters:** `user_id` (REQUIRED \\u2014 numeric customer id or hashid); `duration_days` (integer, REQUIRED). Only active subscriptions can be extended. Response data: `subscription_id`, `user_id`, `product_id`, `duration_extended_days`, `new_completion_date`, `extended_at`, `request_id`.\ \ ---\ \ ## Subscription Proration\ \ > **Note:** These endpoints live on the Seller v1 API (`/api/seller/v1/`) but use the same `x-api-key` authentication as the rest of the Public API. Production base URL: `https://www.fanbasis.com` (with www \\u2014 the apex domain 301-redirects, which drops POST bodies). Sandbox: `https://api-sandbox.commas.net`.\ \ Tier-upgrade endpoints with automatic proration. Three calls drive the flow: list available targets, preview the math, then execute.\ \ ### Get Available Upgrades\ ```\ GET /api/seller/v1/subscriptions/:id/upgrades\ ```\ Lists every valid upgrade target for a subscription with proration math pre-calculated for each.\ \ **Path:** `id` (integer) \\u2014 the subscription record ID.\ \ **Response shape:** `{ subscription_id, subscription_status, current_tier, proration_credit, available_upgrades: [{ service, calculation }] }`. Empty `available_upgrades` array (with 200) when no targets exist or proration isn't enabled.\ \ ### Preview Upgrade\ ```\ GET /api/seller/v1/subscriptions/:id/upgrade/preview?target_service_id=X\ ```\ Proration calculation for one specific target tier \\u2014 read-only, doesn't charge or modify.\ \ **Response shape:** `{ current_service, target_service, price_difference, days_remaining, total_cycle_days, proration_ratio, upgrade_charge, renewal_credit, existing_credit, amount_due, completion_date, available_credit }`.\ \ ### Process Upgrade\ ```\ POST /api/seller/v1/subscriptions/:id/upgrade\ ```\ Executes the upgrade. Marks the original subscription as `upgraded` (stops auto-renewing), creates a new `active` subscription on the target tier, charges immediately or defers.\ \ **Body:** `target_service_id` (integer, required), `billing_mode` (`immediate` | `next_cycle`, defaults to the billing mode configured on your organization), `payment_method_id` (string, required when billing_mode resolves to `immediate`).\ \ **Response shape:** `{ upgrade_id (UUID), base_subscription, new_subscription, calculation, charge: { payment_id, amount_charged, payment_method_type } }`.\ \ **Errors:** `400` upgrade rejected \\u2014 invalid target tier, subscription not active, proration disabled for the organization, downgrade attempted, or stored payment method failed (the body may include a stray `\\\\\\"errors\\\\\\": 422` field; the HTTP status is 400). Real messages: \\"Subscription must be active to upgrade.\\", \\"Proration is not enabled for this seller.\\", \\"Downgrade not allowed. Target tier must have a higher price.\\" `401` bad API key; `404` subscription not found on the GET endpoints, but `POST /upgrade` with a missing or foreign subscription returns `422`, not 404; `500` unhandled processing error. `statuscode` appears on controller errors but NOT on 401/403/422. Proration is organization-level only \\u2014 there is no per-service flag (those columns were dropped) \\u2014 and it is currently force-disabled for organizations processing on Adyen.\ \ **Subscription statuses:** `active` (\\u2705 upgradeable), `past_due`, `upgraded`, `cancelled`, `paused`, `paused_by_admin`, `paused_by_persona`, `holded`, `failed`, `completed`, `onetime_service` (all \\u274c not upgradeable). Each subscription can only be upgraded once \\u2014 call this endpoint on `new_subscription.subscription_id` to upgrade again.\ \ ---\ \ ## Discount Codes\ \ ### List Discount Codes\ ```\ GET /public-api/discount-codes\ ```\ List all discount codes with usage statistics.\ \ **Response fields:** `id` (hashid), `code`, `description`, `type` (`percentage` or `cash`), `value` (string), `duration`, `expiry`, `expiry_time`, `limited_redemptions`, `usable_number`, `one_time`, `code_type`, `service_count`, `usage_count`, `services` (array of `{id, title, price}`). There is no `is_active` field. `search` matches the code, type, value, or attached product title (not the description); `per_page` is not validated server-side.\ \ ### Get Discount Code\ ```\ GET /public-api/discount-codes/:discountCodeId\ ```\ Get details of a specific discount code. `:discountCodeId` must be the HASHID (e.g. `z41y`) \\u2014 a numeric id returns `400 \\"Invalid discount code ID\\"`.\ \ ### Create Discount Code\ ```\ POST /public-api/discount-codes\ ```\ Create a new discount code.\ \ **Body parameters (required):** `code`, `discount_type` (`percentage` or `cash`), `value`, `duration`, `service_ids`. **Optional:** `description`, `expiry`, `limited_redemptions`, `usable_number`, `no_of_months`, `one_time`. Note: the create/update body field is `discount_type`, but GET responses return it as `type`. Success message is \\"Coupon code added successfully.\\"\ \ ### Update Discount Code\ ```\ PUT /public-api/discount-codes/:discountCodeId\ ```\ Update an existing discount code. Full replacement, not a patch \\u2014 send the same required set as create (`code`, `discount_type`, `value`, `duration`, `service_ids`); a partial body returns 400. `:discountCodeId` must be the HASHID.\ \ ### Delete Discount Code\ ```\ DELETE /public-api/discount-codes/:discountCodeId\ ```\ Delete a discount code.\ \ ---\ \ ## Webhooks\ \ ### List Webhook Subscriptions\ ```\ GET /public-api/webhook-subscriptions\ ```\ List all configured webhook endpoints.\ \ ### Create Webhook Subscription\ ```\ POST /public-api/webhook-subscriptions\ ```\ Register a new webhook endpoint.\ \ **Body parameters:** `webhook_url` (uri, required), `event_types` (array \\u2014 valid values: `payment.succeeded`, `payment.failed`, `payment.expired`, `payment.canceled`, `product.purchased`, `subscription.created`, `subscription.renewed`, `subscription.completed`, `subscription.canceled`, `subscription.past_due`, `subscription.recovered`, `refund.created`, `dispute.created`, `dispute.updated`)\ \ The response includes a `secret_key` (`whsk_` + 64 hex characters) for signature validation: each delivery carries an `x-webhook-signature` header containing the hex-encoded HMAC-SHA256 of the raw request body keyed with your `secret_key` — verify with a constant-time compare against the raw bytes (never re-serialized JSON) and reject mismatches with 401. Webhook subscription IDs are plain numeric strings. EVERY real event is delivered envelope-wrapped: `{ \\"id\\": \\" \\", \\"type\\": \\" \\", \\"data\\": { ... }, \\"created_at\\": \\" \\" }` \\u2014 no event is flat on the live path. Payload IDs are public strings (orders `ORD-XXXX-XXXX-XXXX`, buyers `user_` + 12 chars, products/subscriptions/refunds/disputes hashids); amounts are in dollars.\ \ **Delivery semantics:** at-most-once. A failed delivery (non-2xx, timeout, unreachable) is logged and NEVER retried \\u2014 no backoff, no redelivery queue. Dedupe on the envelope `id` and reconcile gaps via the API (e.g. `GET /public-api/checkout-sessions/transactions`) rather than waiting for a retry.\ \ `payment.failed` fires on retries too: every failed dunning/revival attempt inside the recovery window emits its own `payment.failed`, alongside `subscription.past_due`.\ \ ### Delete Webhook Subscription\ ```\ DELETE /public-api/webhook-subscriptions/:webhookSubscriptionId\ ```\ Remove a webhook endpoint.\ \ ### Test Webhook\ ```\ POST /public-api/webhook-subscriptions/:webhookSubscriptionId/test\ ```\ Send a test event to your webhook endpoint.\ \ **Body parameters:** `event_type`\\u2014 The event type to test (e.g., `payment.succeeded`). Three types have no test payload implemented and return 500: `product.purchased`, `subscription.past_due`, `subscription.recovered`. Test deliveries for `payment.succeeded` and the core `subscription.*` events are FLAT (no envelope), unlike real deliveries \\u2014 don't validate a parser against test events alone.\ \ ---\ \ ## Common Response Patterns\ \ **Success:** Returns `200 OK` (or `201` on create) with `{ \\"status\\": \\"success\\", \\"message\\": \\"...\\", \\"data\\": { ... }, \\"request_id\\": \\"...\\" }`. `request_id` is not on every response \\u2014 discount-code and embedded-checkout responses omit it.\ \ **Pagination:** List endpoints return `pagination` inside `data`: `{ \\"current_page\\", \\"total_pages\\", \\"per_page\\", \\"total_items\\", \\"has_more\\" }`.\ \ **Errors:** there is NO machine-readable `code` field \\u2014 branch on HTTP status plus `message`.\ - `400` \\u2014 `{ \\"status\\": \\"error\\", \\"message\\": \\"Validation failed\\", \\"data\\": [], \\"errors\\": { \\"field\\": [\\"msg\\"] } }`. Business-rule failures are also 400s with a descriptive `message` (e.g. \\"Refund amount exceeds remaining refundable amount. Already refunded: $X, Remaining: $Y\\").\ - `401` \\u2014 `{ \\"status\\": \\"error\\", \\"message\\": \\"Authentication required. Please provide X-API-KEY or Bearer token.\\" }`\ - `403` \\u2014 `{ \\"status\\": \\"error\\", \\"message\\": \\"API key does not have permission for this resource. Required scope(s): refunds\\" }`\ - `404` \\u2014 Resource not found (also returned when cancelling a subscription that isn't active)\ - `409` \\u2014 Idempotency-Key replay (charge/refund, 10-minute window)\ - `422` \\u2014 Well-formed but rejected in the resource's current state; `POST /upgrade` with a missing/foreign subscription\ - `429` \\u2014 different envelope: `{ \\"success\\": false, \\"message\\": \\"Too many requests. Please try again later.\\", \\"errors\\": { \\"rate_limit\\": \\"Rate limit exceeded, please try after 60s\\" } }`. Rate limiting applies only to the checkout-sessions and customers groups (10,000 authenticated requests/hour), which are the only groups returning `X-RateLimit-*` headers.\ - `500` \\u2014 Server error\ \ ---\ \ ## Quick Reference\ \ | Endpoint | Method | Description |\ |----------|--------|-------------|\ | `/products` | GET | List all products |\ | `/customers` | GET | Search customers |\ | `/customers/:id/payment-methods` | GET | Customer's saved cards |\ | `/customers/:id/charge` | POST | Charge a customer |\ | `/checkout-sessions` | POST | Create checkout session |\ | DELETE | Delete webhook |\ | `/webhook-subscriptions/:id/test` | POST | Test webhook |\ "; function copyPageAsMarkdown(btn) { navigator.clipboard.writeText(FANBASIS_API_MD).then(function() { var orig = btn.innerHTML; btn.innerHTML = ' Copied!'; btn.style.color = '#166534'; btn.style.background = '#DCFCE7'; document.getElementById('aiDropdownWrap').classList.remove('open'); setTimeout(function() { btn.innerHTML = orig; btn.style.color = ''; btn.style.background = ''; }, 2000); }); } function copyDocsUrl(btn) { navigator.clipboard.writeText('https://raw.githubusercontent.com/otniel-bit/fbnewapi/main/api-docs.md').then(function() { var orig = btn.innerHTML; btn.innerHTML = ' Copied!'; btn.style.background = '#DCFCE7'; btn.style.borderColor = '#86EFAC'; btn.style.color = '#166534'; setTimeout(function() { btn.innerHTML = orig; btn.style.background = 'white'; btn.style.borderColor = 'rgba(var(--accent-rgb),0.4)'; btn.style.color = 'var(--text)'; }, 2000); }); } function switchAITab(platform) { // Update tab buttons document.querySelectorAll('.ai-tab').forEach(function(t) { t.classList.remove('active'); }); var tabBtn = document.getElementById('tab-' + platform); if (tabBtn) tabBtn.classList.add('active'); // Update panels document.querySelectorAll('.ai-tab-panel').forEach(function(p) { p.classList.remove('active'); }); var panel = document.getElementById('panel-' + platform); if (panel) panel.classList.add('active'); } function copyToClip(btn, text) { navigator.clipboard.writeText(text).then(function() { var orig = btn.innerHTML; btn.innerHTML = '✅ Copied!'; btn.classList.add('copied'); setTimeout(function() { btn.innerHTML = orig; btn.classList.remove('copied'); }, 2200); }).catch(function() { // Fallback var ta = document.createElement('textarea'); ta.value = text; ta.style.position = 'fixed'; (function() { var toggle = document.getElementById('themeToggle'); if (!toggle) return; // Remove old click listeners by cloning the button var newToggle = toggle.cloneNode(true); toggle.parentNode.replaceChild(newToggle, toggle); var ICONS = { dark: '☀️', light: '🌙' }; function applyTheme(next) { var html = document.documentElement; html.setAttribute('data-theme', next); try { localStorage.setItem('fb-docs-theme', next); } catch(ex) {} newToggle.textContent = ICONS[next] || '🌙'; newToggle.setAttribute('title', next === 'dark' ? }); // Hide old response elResponse.classList.remove('visible'); updateCurl(); // Scroll builder into view elBuilder.scrollIntoView({ behavior: 'smooth', block: 'start' }); } // ── SEND REQUEST ────────────────────────────────────────── elSendBtn.addEventListener('click', function() { if (!selected) return; }, 2000); return; } // Satisfying pop on the actual send press if (window.fbSound && window.fbSound.pop) window.fbSound.pop(); var url = buildUrl(selected); var opts = { method: selected.m, headers: {} }; This usually means CORS is blocking browser-to-API requests. Copy the cURL command above and paste it into your terminal to make this call directly.'; } // ── HISTORY ─────────────────────────────────────────────── function addHistory(ep, status, ms, reqBody, respBody) { // Collect current path-param and query-param values var ppVals = {}; if (ep.pp && ep.pp.length) { ep.pp.forEach(function(p) { var el = document.getElementById('pg-pp-' + p); if (el) ppVals[p] = el.value; }); } var qpVals = {}; if (ep.qp && ep.qp.length) { ep.qp.forEach(function(p) { var el = document.getElementById('pg-qp-' + p); if (el) qpVals[p] = el.value; }); } // Truncate large response bodies to keep sessionStorage sane (50KB max per entry) var trimResp = (respBody && respBody.length > 51200) ? respBody.substring(0, 51200) + '\ \ … (truncated)' : respBody; history.unshift({ id: ep.id, m: ep.m, path: ep.path, title: ep.title, status: status, ms: ms, ts: Date.now(), reqBody: reqBody || null, respBody: trimResp || null, ppVals: ppVals, qpVals: qpVals }); if (history.length > 10) history = history.slice(0, 10); try { sessionStorage.setItem('pg-history', JSON.stringify(history)); } catch(e) {} renderHistory(); } function renderHistory() { if (!history.length) { elHistList.innerHTML = ' No requests yet — pick an endpoint above and hit Send. '; return; } var html = ''; history.forEach(function(h, i) { var ok = h.status >= 200 && h.status '; html += ' ' + h.m + ' '; html += ' ' + h.title + ' ' + h.path + ' '; html += ' ' + (h.status || 'ERR') + ' '; The log itself is collapsible // in the UI so it never gets in the way. if (typeof checkoutInstance.on === 'function') { var safe = function(d) { try { return typeof d === 'object' ?
