}); // Prefill inputs (nested address fields) section.querySelectorAll('input[data-prefill-address-field]').forEach(function(inp) { inp.value = (state.prefill.address && state.prefill.address[inp.dataset.prefillAddressField]) || ''; }); // Field-control visibility/editability checkboxes — UI labels are // affirmative ("Visible" / "Editable") so the *unchecked* state // maps to the SDK's hide/disable flags. section.querySelectorAll('input[data-field-visible]').forEach(function(cb) { var fc = state.fieldControl[cb.dataset.fieldVisible]; cb.checked = !(fc && fc.hide); }); section.querySelectorAll('input[data-field-editable]').forEach(function(cb) { var fc = state.fieldControl[cb.dataset.fieldEditable]; cb.checked = !(fc && fc.disable); }); } // ─── Toggle reconciliation ─── // Reflects state.features in the UI: shows/hides the Add-ons + Prefill // drawer tabs, the parent-page addon cards, the external Pay button, // and reconciles the layout hint. The event log is always visible // (collapsible) — it's no longer gated by the programmatic feature. function applyToggles() { var f = state.features; // External Pay button slot below the iframe var progSlot = document.getElementById('sdkd-prog-slot'); if (progSlot) progSlot.hidden = !f.programmatic; // Add-ons drawer tab + helper cards above iframe var prodTab = section.querySelector('.sdkd-tab-products'); if (prodTab) { if (f.addons) prodTab.removeAttribute('hidden'); else prodTab.setAttribute('hidden', ''); } if (f.addons) renderAddonHelper(); else $helper.innerHTML = ''; // Prefill drawer tab var prefillTab = section.querySelector('.sdkd-tab-prefill'); if (prefillTab) { if (f.prefill) prefillTab.removeAttribute('hidden'); else prefillTab.setAttribute('hidden', ''); } // Note: product_info layout controls stay editable when Add-ons is // on — the user can re-enable the iframe product hero on top of the // parent-page addon cards if they want both. var addonHint = section.querySelector('.sdkd-addon-hint'); if (addonHint) addonHint.hidden = !f.addons; // If the user was on a tab that just got hidden, fall back to Colors var activeTab = section.querySelector('.sdkd-cust-tab.active'); if (activeTab && activeTab.hasAttribute('hidden')) activateCustTab('colors'); } function activateCustTab(name) { section.querySelectorAll('.sdkd-cust-tab').forEach(function(t) { t.classList.toggle('active', t.dataset.custTab === name); }); section.querySelectorAll('.sdkd-cust-panel').forEach(function(p) { p.classList.toggle('active', p.dataset.custPanel === name); }); } // Renders the live parent-page UI (addon cards) above the SDK iframe // when the Add-ons feature is on. Each card is wired to // checkout.addAddon / removeAddon so users see the SDK iframe respond. function renderAddonHelper() { if (!state.features.addons) return; var checkSvg = ' '; var addonCards = state.addons.map(function(a, i) { var p = '$' + (Number(a.price) || 0).toFixed(2).replace(/\\B(?=(\\d{3})+(?!\\d))/g, ','); return ( ' ' + ' ' + checkSvg + ' ' + ' ' + ' ' + escHtml(a.name) + ' ' + (a.desc ? ' ' + escHtml(a.desc) + ' ' : '') + ' ' + ' +' + p + ' ' + ' ' ); }).join(''); $helper.innerHTML = ' Choose your add-ons ' + (addonCards ? ' ' + addonCards + ' ' : ' No add-ons yet — open the Products tab to add some. '); // Wire card clicks (+ keyboard) to the live SDK instance and track // selected state for visual feedback. var selectedSet = {}; $helper.querySelectorAll('.sdkd-helper-card').forEach(function(card) { var id = card.dataset.helperAddonId; var toggle = function() { var nowSelected = !selectedSet[id]; selectedSet[id] = nowSelected; card.classList.toggle('selected', nowSelected); if (!checkoutInstance) return; try { if (nowSelected) { checkoutInstance.addAddon(id); logEvent('addAddon()', id); } else { checkoutInstance.removeAddon(id); logEvent('removeAddon()', id); } } catch (e) { console.error('[addon helper] toggle failed', e); } }; card.addEventListener('click', toggle); card.addEventListener('keydown', function(e) { if (e.key === ' ' || e.key === 'Enter') { e.preventDefault(); toggle(); } }); }); } function escHtml(s) { return String(s == null ? '' : s).replace(/&/g, ' ').replace(/ /g, ' '); } function escAttr(s) { return String(s == null ? '' : s).replace(/&/g, ' ').replace(/"/g, ' ').replace(/'/g, ' '); } // ─── iFrame v2: addon list editor ─── function renderAddons() { var list = document.getElementById('sdkd-addons-list'); if (!list) return; list.innerHTML = ''; state.addons.forEach(function(addon, idx) { var item = document.createElement('div'); item.className = 'sdkd-addon-item'; item.innerHTML = ' × ' + ' Addon ID ' + ' Name ' + ' Price (USD) ' + ' Description '; list.appendChild(item); // Set values item.querySelector('[data-addon-field="id"]').value = addon.id; item.querySelector('[data-addon-field="name"]').value = addon.name; item.querySelector('[data-addon-field="price"]').value = addon.price; item.querySelector('[data-addon-field="desc"]').value = addon.desc; }); // Wire inputs (delegate via direct binding since list rebuilds) list.querySelectorAll('input[data-addon-field]').forEach(function(inp) { inp.addEventListener('input', function() { var idx = parseInt(inp.dataset.addonIdx, 10); var field = inp.dataset.addonField; if (!state.addons[idx]) return; state.addons[idx][field] = field === 'price' ? parseFloat(inp.value) || 0 : inp.value; renderAddonHelper(); refreshSnippet(); }); }); list.querySelectorAll('[data-addon-rm]').forEach(function(btn) { btn.addEventListener('click', function() { var idx = parseInt(btn.dataset.addonRm, 10); state.addons.splice(idx, 1); renderAddons(); renderAddonHelper(); refreshSnippet(); }); }); } function syncProductInputs() { section.querySelectorAll('input[data-prod-field], textarea[data-prod-field]').forEach(function(el) { var k = el.dataset.prodField; if (state.product[k] !== undefined) el.value = state.product[k]; }); } // ─── Preset application ─── // applyPresetState: pure state mutator (no UI/scheduling side effects) // so recipes can compose presets with extra state changes and run the // UI pipeline once at the end. function applyPresetState(name) { var p = PRESETS[name]; if (!p) return; Object.keys(p).forEach(function(k) { state[k] = p[k]; }); } function applyPreset(name) { applyPresetState(name); syncControlsFromState(); refreshSnippet(); scheduleMount(); } // resetTransientState: returns features + field controls + prefill + // product_info display back to DEFAULTS. Used by applyRecipe so each // recipe starts from a clean baseline rather than inheriting whatever // the previous recipe set. function resetTransientState() { state.features = Object.assign({}, FEATURE_DEFAULTS); Object.keys(FIELD_CONTROL_DEFAULTS).forEach(function(k) { state.fieldControl[k] = Object.assign({}, FIELD_CONTROL_DEFAULTS[k]); }); state.prefill = Object.assign({}, PREFILL_DEFAULT); if (PREFILL_DEFAULT.address) state.prefill.address = Object.assign({}, PREFILL_DEFAULT.address); state.show_product_info = DEFAULTS.show_product_info; state.product_layout = DEFAULTS.product_layout; state.collectPhone = DEFAULTS.collectPhone; } function applyRecipe(id) { var r = RECIPES[id]; if (!r) return; resetTransientState(); r.apply(); syncControlsFromState(); applyToggles(); refreshSnippet(); scheduleMount(); } // ─── Reset ─── function resetAll() { Object.assign(state, DEFAULTS); state.fields = Object.assign({}, DEFAULTS.fields); state.product = Object.assign({}, PRODUCT_DEFAULTS); state.addons = ADDON_DEFAULTS.map(function(a) { return Object.assign({}, a); }); state.prefill = Object.assign({}, PREFILL_DEFAULT); state.features = Object.assign({}, FEATURE_DEFAULTS); state.fieldControl = {}; Object.keys(FIELD_CONTROL_DEFAULTS).forEach(function(k) { state.fieldControl[k] = Object.assign({}, FIELD_CONTROL_DEFAULTS[k]); }); syncControlsFromState(); syncProductInputs(); renderAddons(); applyToggles(); refreshSnippet(); scheduleMount(); } // ─── Tab switching (Customize) ─── section.querySelectorAll('.sdkd-cust-tab').forEach(function(tab) { tab.addEventListener('click', function() { section.querySelectorAll('.sdkd-cust-tab').forEach(function(t) { t.classList.toggle('active', t === tab); }); var which = tab.dataset.custTab; section.querySelectorAll('.sdkd-cust-panel').forEach(function(p) { p.classList.toggle('active', p.dataset.custPanel === which); }); }); }); // ─── Wire all controls ─── // Feature toggles — wired on every input[data-feature], which covers // both the toolbar pill chips AND the drawer Layout-tab checkboxes. // syncControlsFromState() at the end keeps the two surfaces in lockstep. section.querySelectorAll('input[data-feature]').forEach(function(cb) { cb.addEventListener('change', function() { if (cb.checked) playToggleOn(); else playToggleOff(); var key = cb.dataset.feature; var wasOn = !!state.features[key]; state.features[key] = cb.checked; // Add-ons: turning ON defaults product info to hidden (parent page // owns the product hero). Turning OFF restores the original // show/layout defaults. The Layout controls stay live in both // cases, so the user can override either default. if (key === 'addons' && cb.checked && !wasOn) { state.show_product_info = false; state.product_layout = 'none'; } else if (key === 'addons' && !cb.checked && wasOn) { state.show_product_info = DEFAULTS.show_product_info; state.product_layout = DEFAULTS.product_layout; } syncControlsFromState(); applyToggles(); refreshSnippet(); scheduleMount(); }); }); // Prefill field inputs (top-level) section.querySelectorAll('input[data-prefill-field]').forEach(function(inp) { inp.addEventListener('input', function() { state.prefill[inp.dataset.prefillField] = inp.value; refreshSnippet(); scheduleMount(); }); }); // Prefill address inputs (nested address object) section.querySelectorAll('input[data-prefill-address-field]').forEach(function(inp) { inp.addEventListener('input', function() { if (!state.prefill.address) state.prefill.address = {}; state.prefill.address[inp.dataset.prefillAddressField] = inp.value; refreshSnippet(); scheduleMount(); }); }); // Per-field Visible / Editable toggles — drive theme.fields.{x} section.querySelectorAll('input[data-field-visible]').forEach(function(cb) { cb.addEventListener('change', function() { var k = cb.dataset.fieldVisible; if (!state.fieldControl[k]) state.fieldControl[k] = { hide: false, disable: false }; state.fieldControl[k].hide = !cb.checked; refreshSnippet(); scheduleMount(); }); }); section.querySelectorAll('input[data-field-editable]').forEach(function(cb) { cb.addEventListener('change', function() { var k = cb.dataset.fieldEditable; if (!state.fieldControl[k]) state.fieldControl[k] = { hide: false, disable: false }; state.fieldControl[k].disable = !cb.checked; refreshSnippet(); scheduleMount(); }); }); // Color pickers + paired text inputs section.querySelectorAll('input[data-cfg-color]').forEach(function(inp) { inp.addEventListener('input', function() { var k = inp.dataset.cfgColor; state[k] = inp.value; var twin = section.querySelector('input[data-cfg-text="' + k + '"]'); if (twin) twin.value = inp.value.toUpperCase(); refreshSnippet(); scheduleMount(); }); }); section.querySelectorAll('input[data-cfg-text]').forEach(function(inp) { inp.addEventListener('change', function() { var k = inp.dataset.cfgText; var v = (inp.value || '').trim(); if (!/^#[0-9a-fA-F]{6}$/.test(v)) { inp.value = (state[k] || '').toUpperCase(); return; } state[k] = v.toLowerCase(); var twin = section.querySelector('input[data-cfg-color="' + k + '"]'); if (twin) twin.value = state[k]; refreshSnippet(); scheduleMount(); }); }); // Layout selects section.querySelectorAll('select[data-cfg-select]').forEach(function(sel) { sel.addEventListener('change', function() { var key = sel.dataset.cfgSelect; state[key] = sel.value; // Couple the product info controls: picking 'none' for layout // implicitly turns off Show Product Info; picking left/right // implicitly turns it on. Keeps the two controls in sync. if (key === 'product_layout') { state.show_product_info = (sel.value !== 'none'); syncControlsFromState(); } refreshSnippet(); scheduleMount(); }); }); // Boolean checkboxes section.querySelectorAll('input[data-cfg-bool]').forEach(function(cb) { cb.addEventListener('change', function() { var key = cb.dataset.cfgBool; state[key] = cb.checked; // Couple Show Product Info ↔ product_layout. Checking it while the // layout is 'none' bumps the layout back to 'left' (most common // default). Unchecking leaves the layout alone — the dual-off // logic in buildConfig handles the actual hide. if (key === 'show_product_info' && cb.checked && state.product_layout === 'none') { state.product_layout = 'left'; syncControlsFromState(); } refreshSnippet(); scheduleMount(); }); }); // Field checkboxes section.querySelectorAll('input[data-cfg-field]').forEach(function(cb) { cb.addEventListener('change', function() { state.fields[cb.dataset.cfgField] = cb.checked; refreshSnippet(); scheduleMount(); }); }); // Presets section.querySelectorAll('.sdkd-preset').forEach(function(btn) { btn.addEventListener('click', function() { playPop(); applyPreset(btn.dataset.preset); }); }); // Recipes (toolbar chips) — bundle of features + preset + field controls section.querySelectorAll('.sdkd-recipe').forEach(function(btn) { btn.addEventListener('click', function() { playPop(); applyRecipe(btn.dataset.recipe); }); }); // Product field inputs (Add-on variant) section.querySelectorAll('input[data-prod-field], textarea[data-prod-field]').forEach(function(el) { el.addEventListener('input', function() { var k = el.dataset.prodField; state.product[k] = (k === 'price') ? (parseFloat(el.value) || 0) : el.value; renderAddonHelper(); refreshSnippet(); }); }); // Addon add button var addonAddBtn = document.getElementById('sdkd-addon-add'); if (addonAddBtn) { addonAddBtn.addEventListener('click', function() { state.addons.push({ id: 'addon_id_' + (state.addons.length + 1), name: 'New addon', price: 0, desc: 'Describe what this addon includes.' }); renderAddons(); renderAddonHelper(); refreshSnippet(); }); } // Reset $resetBtn.addEventListener('click', resetAll); // Event-log clear (stop propagation so the click doesn't also toggle // the collapse on the parent header). if ($eventClear) $eventClear.addEventListener('click', function(e) { e.stopPropagation(); $eventBody.innerHTML = ''; eventCount = 0; var $c = document.getElementById('sdkd-event-log-count'); if ($c) $c.textContent = '0 events'; }); // Event-log collapse — click the header (or Enter/Space) to toggle. // Persists in localStorage so the user's choice survives reloads. var $eventToggle = document.getElementById('sdkd-event-log-toggle'); function setEventLogCollapsed(collapsed) { $eventLog.classList.toggle('collapsed', collapsed); if ($eventToggle) $eventToggle.setAttribute('aria-expanded', collapsed ? 'false' : 'true'); try { localStorage.setItem('sdkd-eventlog-collapsed', collapsed ? '1' : '0'); } catch (e) {} } if ($eventToggle) { $eventToggle.addEventListener('click', function() { setEventLogCollapsed(!$eventLog.classList.contains('collapsed')); }); $eventToggle.addEventListener('keydown', function(e) { if (e.key === 'Enter' || e.key === ' ') { e.preventDefault(); setEventLogCollapsed(!$eventLog.classList.contains('collapsed')); } }); } // Restore previous collapse state on first load try { var stored = localStorage.getItem('sdkd-eventlog-collapsed'); if (stored === '0') setEventLogCollapsed(false); } catch (e) {} // ─── Sound engine (Web Audio API, generated tones) ─── // Tasteful pops + ticks on key interactions. Lazy-init the audio // context on first user gesture (browser policy). User can mute // via a 🔊 button in the toolbar; choice persists in localStorage. // Respects prefers-reduced-motion (defaults off when set). var soundEnabled = true; try { var soundPref = localStorage.getItem('sdkd-sound'); if (soundPref === 'off') soundEnabled = false; else if (soundPref === null && window.matchMedia && window.matchMedia('(prefers-reduced-motion: reduce)').matches) { soundEnabled = false; } } catch (e) {} var _audioCtx = null; function getAudioCtx() { if (_audioCtx) return _audioCtx; try { var AC = window.AudioContext || window.webkitAudioContext; if (!AC) return null; _audioCtx = new AC(); return _audioCtx; } catch (e) { return null; } } function playTone(opts) { if (!soundEnabled) return; var ctx = getAudioCtx(); if (!ctx) return; if (ctx.state === 'suspended') { try { ctx.resume(); } catch (e) {} } var now = ctx.currentTime; var dur = opts.duration || 0.08; var osc = ctx.createOscillator(); var gain = ctx.createGain(); osc.connect(gain); gain.connect(ctx.destination); osc.type = opts.type || 'sine'; osc.frequency.setValueAtTime(opts.freqStart, now); if (opts.freqEnd && opts.freqEnd !== opts.freqStart) { osc.frequency.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(Math.max(50, opts.freqEnd), now + dur); } var vol = opts.volume || 0.06; gain.gain.setValueAtTime(0, now); gain.gain.linearRampToValueAtTime(vol, now + 0.005); gain.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(0.0001, now + dur); osc.start(now); osc.stop(now + dur + 0.02); } function playPop() { playTone({ freqStart: 600, freqEnd: 380, duration: 0.09, volume: 0.06 }); } function playTick() { playTone({ freqStart: 1200, duration: 0.04, volume: 0.04, type: 'triangle' }); } function playToggleOn() { playTone({ freqStart: 440, freqEnd: 880, duration: 0.10, volume: 0.06 }); } function playToggleOff() { playTone({ freqStart: 660, freqEnd: 330, duration: 0.10, volume: 0.05 }); } function playDing() { // Two-note success — root + perfect fifth, very short attack playTone({ freqStart: 880, duration: 0.18, volume: 0.05 }); playTone({ freqStart: 1320, duration: 0.18, volume: 0.035 }); } function playError() { // Falling minor — a soft "nope" without being harsh playTone({ freqStart: 440, freqEnd: 220, duration: 0.22, volume: 0.05, type: 'triangle' }); } // Expose the sound engine globally so other docs sections (e.g. the // API Playground) can use the same palette + share the mute state. // Defensive call sites should still use `window.fbSound && fbSound.x()`. window.fbSound = { pop: playPop, tick: playTick, toggleOn: playToggleOn, toggleOff: playToggleOff, ding: playDing, error: playError, isEnabled: function() { return soundEnabled; }, setEnabled: function(v) { soundEnabled = !!v; try { localStorage.setItem('sdkd-sound', soundEnabled ? 'on' : 'off'); } catch (e) {} if (typeof refreshSoundBtn === 'function') refreshSoundBtn(); } }; // Sound toggle buttons (SDK Demo toolbar + API Playground env-bar). // refreshSoundBtn walks ALL .sdkd-sound-btn so toggling one syncs the // visual state of every copy. Click handler is the same for each. function refreshSoundBtn() { document.querySelectorAll('.sdkd-sound-btn').forEach(function(btn) { btn.textContent = soundEnabled ? '🔊' : '🔇'; btn.setAttribute('aria-pressed', soundEnabled ? 'true' : 'false'); btn.setAttribute('title', soundEnabled ? 'Click sounds on (click to mute)' : 'Click sounds off (click to enable)'); }); } document.querySelectorAll('.sdkd-sound-btn').forEach(function(btn) { btn.addEventListener('click', function() { soundEnabled = !soundEnabled; try { localStorage.setItem('sdkd-sound', soundEnabled ? 'on' : 'off'); } catch (e) {} refreshSoundBtn(); if (soundEnabled) playToggleOn(); // confirm the unmute audibly }); }); refreshSoundBtn(); // ─── Viewport preview toggle ─── // Lets the user constrain the mount container to phone/tablet // widths so they can preview their checkout at a smaller size // without leaving their desktop. Selection persists in localStorage. var $preview = document.getElementById('sdkd-preview'); var $vpBtns = section.querySelectorAll('.sdkd-vp-btn'); function setViewport(vp) { if (!$preview) return; $preview.setAttribute('data-viewport', vp); $vpBtns.forEach(function(b) { b.classList.toggle('active', b.dataset.viewport === vp); }); try { localStorage.setItem('sdkd-viewport', vp); } catch (e) {} } $vpBtns.forEach(function(b) { b.addEventListener('click', function() { playTick(); setViewport(b.dataset.viewport); }); }); try { var storedVp = localStorage.getItem('sdkd-viewport'); if (storedVp === 'mobile' || storedVp === 'tablet') setViewport(storedVp); } catch (e) {} // ─── Test cards click-to-copy ─── // The iframe is cross-origin so we can't programmatically fill the // card field. Best UX: copy the PAN to the clipboard, flash a // "Copied!" pill, let the user paste into the card input. function copyPan(btn) { var pan = btn.dataset.pan; var done = function() { playDing(); btn.classList.add('copied'); setTimeout(function() { btn.classList.remove('copied'); }, 1800); }; if (navigator.clipboard && navigator.clipboard.writeText) { navigator.clipboard.writeText(pan).then(done).catch(function() { // Fallback for browsers without async clipboard fallbackCopy(pan); done(); }); } else { fallbackCopy(pan); done(); } } section.querySelectorAll('.sdkd-testcard').forEach(function(btn) { btn.addEventListener('click', function() { copyPan(btn); }); }); // ─── Shareable URLs ─── // Encode current state into the URL hash so a recipient opening // the link sees the exact same SDK Demo configuration. // Hash format: #sdk-demo?cfg= function getSerializableState() { return { v: 1, // schema version theme: state.theme, accent_color: state.accent_color, background_color: state.background_color, surface_color: state.surface_color, heading_color: state.heading_color, label_color: state.label_color, product_text_color: state.product_text_color, secondary_color: state.secondary_color, input_background_color: state.input_background_color, border_color: state.border_color, product_layout: state.product_layout, billing_form_placement: state.billing_form_placement, show_product_info: state.show_product_info, show_coupon_row: state.show_coupon_row, fields: Object.assign({}, state.fields), collectPhone: state.collectPhone, features: Object.assign({}, state.features), fieldControl: JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(state.fieldControl)), prefill: JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(state.prefill)), addons: state.addons.map(function(a) { return Object.assign({}, a); }) }; } function encodeState() { try { var json = JSON.stringify(getSerializableState()); // URL-safe base64 return btoa(unescape(encodeURIComponent(json))) .replace(/\\+/g, '-').replace(/\\//g, '_').replace(/=+$/, ''); } catch (e) { return ''; } } function decodeState(b64) { try { var raw = b64.replace(/-/g, '+').replace(/_/g, '/'); var pad = raw.length % 4; if (pad) raw += '===='.slice(pad); return JSON.parse(decodeURIComponent(escape(atob(raw)))); } catch (e) { return null; } } function applyDecodedState(s) { if (!s || s.v !== 1) return false; // Shallow-apply top-level scalar fields ['theme','accent_color','background_color','surface_color','heading_color', 'label_color','product_text_color','secondary_color','input_background_color', 'border_color','product_layout','billing_form_placement','show_product_info', 'show_coupon_row','collectPhone'].forEach(function(k) { if (s[k] !== undefined) state[k] = s[k]; }); if (s.fields) state.fields = Object.assign({}, state.fields, s.fields); if (s.features) state.features = Object.assign({}, state.features, s.features); if (s.fieldControl) { Object.keys(s.fieldControl).forEach(function(k) { state.fieldControl[k] = Object.assign({}, state.fieldControl[k], s.fieldControl[k]); }); } if (s.prefill) { state.prefill = Object.assign({}, state.prefill, s.prefill); if (s.prefill.address) state.prefill.address = Object.assign({}, state.prefill.address, s.prefill.address); } if (Array.isArray(s.addons) && s.addons.length) { state.addons = s.addons.map(function(a) { return Object.assign({}, a); }); renderAddons(); } syncControlsFromState(); applyToggles(); refreshSnippet(); scheduleMount(); return true; } // Wire the Share button var $shareBtn = document.getElementById('sdkd-share-btn'); if ($shareBtn) { $shareBtn.addEventListener('click', function() { var url = window.location.origin + window.location.pathname + '?cfg=' + encodeState() + '#sdk-demo'; var done = function() { playDing(); $shareBtn.classList.add('copied'); var orig = $shareBtn.textContent; $shareBtn.textContent = '✓ Link copied!'; setTimeout(function() { $shareBtn.classList.remove('copied'); $shareBtn.textContent = orig; }, 2000); }; if (navigator.clipboard && navigator.clipboard.writeText) { navigator.clipboard.writeText(url).then(done).catch(function() { fallbackCopy(url); done(); }); } else { fallbackCopy(url); done(); } }); } // ─── Snippet language tabs ─── // Switch the snippet between HTML / React / Vue / Next.js / cURL. // Same config, language-appropriate idiom. Selection persists. var $langLabel = document.getElementById('sdkd-lang-label'); var $langTabs = section.querySelectorAll('.sdkd-lang-tab'); function setLanguage(lang) { if (['html','react','vue','next','curl'].indexOf(lang) /* ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════ a11y-enhancer.js — runtime accessibility semantics for the Commas API docs. Standalone, ES5-style, no build step, no dependencies. Applies the "mass" fixes that would otherwise need hundreds of markup edits: table header scopes, decorative-icon hiding, aria-current sync, tab semantics for injected tab bars, focusable scrolling code blocks, copy-to-clipboard announcements, generated form-field label pairing, Cmd+K listbox semantics, and inert/aria- hidden sync for the mobile sidebar. Every query is null-checked; nothing here changes visual rendering. ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════ */ (function () { 'use strict'; if (typeof document === 'undefined' || typeof window === 'undefined') return; if (window.__fbA11yEnhancerLoaded) return; window.__fbA11yEnhancerLoaded = true; /* ── helpers ──────────────────────────────────────────────────── */ function qs(sel, root) { try { return (root || document).querySelector(sel); } catch (e) { return null; } } function qsa(sel, root) { try { return Array.prototype.slice.call((root || document).querySelectorAll(sel)); } catch (e) { return []; } } // querySelectorAll that also includes `root` itself when it matches function qsaIncl(sel, root) { var list = qsa(sel, root); try { if (root && root.nodeType === 1 && root.matches && root.matches(sel)) list.unshift(root); } catch (e) {} return list; } function setAttr(el, name, value) { if (!el || !el.setAttribute) return; value = String(value); if (el.getAttribute(name) !== value) el.setAttribute(name, value); } function hasVisibleText(el) { if (!el) return false; var t = el.textContent || ''; return t.replace(/\\s+/g, '').length > 0; } function textOf(el) { return (el && el.textContent ? Tables: header scopes + empty corner header ───────────── */ function enhanceTables(root) { qsaIncl('thead th', root).forEach(function (th) { if (!th.getAttribute('scope')) th.setAttribute('scope', 'col'); }); qsaIncl('table.mor-compare', root).forEach(function (table) { var corner = qs('thead tr > th:first-child', table); if (corner && !hasVisibleText(corner) && !qs('.sr-only', corner)) { var label = document.createElement('span'); label.className = 'sr-only'; label.textContent = 'Feature'; corner.appendChild(label); } qsa('tbody tr', table).forEach(function (tr) { var first = tr.children && tr.children[0]; if (first && first.tagName === 'TH' && !first.getAttribute('scope')) first.setAttribute('scope', 'row'); }); }); } /* ── 2.
